def stable_smooth_logsum(c, alpha):
 m=np.max(c); shift=alpha*(c-m); mx=np.max(shift); if mx>500: val=m+(mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shift-mx))))/alpha else: val=m+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shift)))? Actually if mx large? More stable: logsum = mx + log(sum exp(shift-mx)); val = m + logsum/alpha. Always use.
return val, w = exp(shift-mx); w /= sum.

def c5_full(h,n):
 dx=2/n
 c=np.empty(2*n-1)
 one_minus=1-h
 for m in range(n):
   L=n-m
   if L>0:
     c[n-1+m] = dx*np.dot(h[:L], one_minus[m:]) # lag +m
 for m in range(1,n):
   L=n-m
   c[n-1-m] = dx*np.dot(h[m:], one_minus[:L]) # lag -m
 return c

   val, _ = smooth_value(c, alpha)
   # box penalty: p = q*sum over (0.5*(u-1)^2 when u>1 + (u+1)^2 when u<-1)
   over = np.maximum(0.0, u-1.0); under=np.maximum(0.0, -1.0-u); val += q*0.25? Since h violation =0.5*over. Squared h viol =0.25(over^2+under^2). Use q*0.25 sum.
   return float(val)
Gradient:
   grad_h = grad_smooth_max(c,n,alpha)
   dP/du = q*0.25*(2*over -2*under) =0.5q(over-under)
   grad_u = 0.5*q*(over-under) (shape n)
   grad = 0.5*B.T@(grad_h + grad_u)
   return grad

```
import numpy as np, time, scipy.optimize as opt

def c5_values(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')*(2.0/n)
def c5_max(h,n): return float(np.max(c5_values(h,n)))

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2
 for _ in range(200):
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
  if diff>0:
   room=1-h; total=room.sum(); if total>0: h += diff*room/total
  else:
   total=h.sum(); if total>0: h += diff*h/total
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # final exact? Could have floating error. Use a final adjustment to one entry? But sum may not exactly n/2. Use high precision? The evaluation sum*h dx=1 requires sum exactly? Harness may check with tolerance. We can adjust by adding residual to many nonzero entries. Do while? For safety:
 h=(np.round(h*1e12)/1e12?) no. We'll do a final Karmarkar: after loop, residual=target-h.sum(); if nonzero distribute to indices not at bound proportionally. Use same update once. Floating okay.
 return h
```

def build_basis(n, M):
 i=np.arange(n)
 ang=2*np.pi*(i+0.5)/n
 B=np.empty((n,M))
 for m in range(M): B[:,m]=np.cos((m+1)*ang)
 return B
def lowdim_h(B,a): u=B@a; return 0.5+0.5*u, u
def lowdim_obj(x, B, n, alpha, q):
 u=B@x; h=0.5+0.5*u
 c=c5_values(h,n)
 val,_=smooth_value(c,alpha)
 over=np.maximum(0,u-1); under=np.maximum(0,-1-u)
 val += 0.25*q*np.dot(over,over)+...
 return val
def lowdim_jac(x, B, n, alpha, q):
 u=B@x; h=0.5+0.5*u
 c=c5_values(h,n); _,w=smooth_weights(c,alpha)
 gh=grad_smooth_from_w(c or h,n,alpha,w) maybe implement takes c,h,alpha.
 over... du=0.5*q*(over-under)
 return 0.5*B.T@(gh+du)
```

def smooth_weights(c,alpha):

```

def local_pair(h,n,deadline,K=12,deltas=(0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001)):
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_max
 alphas=[2000,10000,50000,200000]
 for alpha in alphas:
   while time<deadline:
     c=c5_vec(best_h,n); _,w=smooth_val_w(c,alpha); g=grad_smooth(c,best_h,n,alpha,w)
     # To preserve sum, moving from high g to low g? If we move δ from j to i, first order δ(g_i - g_j). Want negative -> i low, j high.
     low=np.argpartition(g,K)[:K]
     high=np.argpartition(-g,K)[:K]
     improved=False
     for i in low:
       for j in high:
         if i==j: continue
         for d in deltas:
          if best_h[i]+d <=1 and best_h[j]-d >=0:
            old_i=best_h[i]; old_j=best_h[j]
            best_h[i]+=d; best_h[j]-=d
            cnew=c5_max(best_h,n)
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-12:
              best_c=cnew; improved=True
              break
            else:
              best_h[i]=old_i; best_h[j]=old_j
         # maybe if improved allow continue? Recompute c? We'll break after each accepted to update outer? Simpler set a flag and after scan break while to recompute gradient.
     if not improved: break
     # To recompute, continue while (small). But if many accepts, gradient stale. We can counter accepted; if accepted 5 break.
     if improved and accepted_count>=5: break
 return best_h,best_c
```
Need track accepted. If h modified, best_c updated. We can break after any accept to recompute gradient (conservative but slower). n small. Use accepted_count=0; when accept, accepted_count+=1; if accepted_count>=3 break loops. Implement nested loops with flag `do_break`.

def c5_vec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)

`smooth_val_w(c, alpha):`
```
def smooth_val_w(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    s = np.exp(sh - mx)
    ss = float(s.sum())
    if ss <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        w = np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0; return m, w
    w = s / ss
    val = m + (mx + np.log(ss)) / alpha
    return float(val), w
```
Since m max, sh max=0, mx=0, okay.
`grad_smooth(c, h, n, alpha, w=None):`
```
def grad_smooth(c, h, n, alpha, w=None):
    if w is None:
        _, w = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    dx = 2.0/n
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    off=n-1
    for idx in range(2*n-1):
        wi=w[idx]
        if wi < 1e-15: continue
        p=idx-off
        q=abs(p); L=n-q
        wm=wi*dx
        if L<=0: continue
        if p>=0:
            grad[q:q+L] += wm*(1.0-h[:L])
            grad[:L] += -wm*h[q:q+L]
        else:
            grad[:L] += wm*(1.0-h[q:q+L])
            grad[q:q+L] += -wm*h[:L]
    return grad
```
Check p=0: q=0,L=n; p>=0 branch: grad[0:n]+=(1-h), grad[0:n]+= -h -> (1-2h). Good.
`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(200):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=1e-15: break
          h += diff*room/tot
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<=1e-15: break
          h += diff*h/tot
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
Could after final, sum not exact due clip; but okay. We can one more if residual: find indices not at bounds and distribute. But loops handle; if diff after clip maybe. Add:
```
for _ in range(10):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    # adjust first n indices by diff/n if possible? May violate. Use room or h.
```
Okay.

`build_basis(n,M):`
```
def build_basis(n,M):
    M=max(1,int(M))
    i=np.arange(n)+0.5
    ang=2.0*np.pi*i/n
    B=np.zeros((n,M))
    col=0
    m=1
    while col<M and m*2 < n: # avoid alias? m < n/2
        if col<M: B[:,col]=np.cos(m*ang); col+=1
        if col<M: B[:,col]=np.sin(m*ang); col+=1
        m+=1
    return B[:, :col]
```
For n=300, m up to150. If M large, okay. If returns cols <M (if n small), adjust in lowdim.

def full_jac(x,n,alpha,mu):

Local pair top implementation:
```
def local_pair(h,n,deadline):
 best=h.copy(); best_c=c5_max(best,n)
 alphas=[2000,10000,50000]
 deltas=[0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002]
 K=8
 for alpha in alphas:
  while time.time()<deadline:
   c=c5_vec(best,n); _,w=smooth_val_w(c,alpha); g=grad_smooth(c,best,n,alpha,w)
   low=np.argpartition(g,K)[:K] if n>K else np.arange(n)
   high=np.argpartition(-g,K)[:K] if n>K else np.arange(n)
   improved=False; accepts=0
   for i in low:
    if accepts>0 and accepts>=3: break
    for j in high:
      if i==j: continue
      for d in deltas:
       hi=best[i]+d; lo=best[j]-d
       if hi<=1.0 and lo>=0.0:
          oi=best[i]; oj=best[j]; best[i]=hi; best[j]=lo
          cn=c5_max(best,n)
          if cn < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=cn; improved=True; accepts+=1
             break
          else:
             best[i]=oi; best[j]=oj
      if accepts>=3: break
   if not improved: break
   # if improved, loop recomputes
 return best,best_c
```
This uses current best_c but if accepts modified, c vector at next while recomputed. Good. If no improvement, move to next alpha.
Could also try reverse moves (low -d, high +d) where gradient suggests moving from low to high? Since if g_i -g_j? We define moving from j to i (increase i, decrease j) first order δ(g_i-g_j). If g_i < g_j (low high), change negative. Good. What if g_i>g_j but moving opposite? That would be covered by low/high swapped? If low index g high? No. But due quadratic, reverse move could help; include both directions? We can in loop try d positive (i +=, j -=) and if not improve after all deltas, try reverse? For pairs low/high, reverse would likely increase first-order, but may be useful. Skip.
Maybe choose `i` from low, `j` from high. K small.

At run final:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(10,budget_s)-5
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 best_h=None; best_c=inf; best_n=None
 # init
 try: h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
 except: h0=None; n0=300
 if h0 is not None and len(h0)>0:
   if 60 <= n0 <= 600:
      n_use=n0; best_h=project_feasible(h0,n_use); best_c=c5_max(best_h,n_use); best_n=n_use
   else:
      n_use=300
      xold=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n0); xnew=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_use)
      best_h=project_feasible(np.interp(xnew,xold,h0),n_use); best_c=c5_max(best_h,n_use); best_n=n_use
 else:
   n_use=300; best_h=None; best_n=n_use
 # pure cos if time
 if best_h is None or time.time() < deadline-400:
   t_pure = min(time.time()+40, deadline-300)
   a1,h_p,c_p=opt_pure_cos(n_use,t_pure)
   if best_h is None: best_h=h_p; best_c=c_p; best_n=n_use
   elif c_p < best_c: update
 # lowdim if time
 if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline-350:
   t_low = min(time.time()+250, deadline-250)
   h_l,c_l = lowdim_refine(n_use,best_h,a1,t_low)
   if c_l < best_c: update
 # full refine on best
 if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline-150:
   h_f,c_f=lbfgs_full(best_h,best_n,deadline-180)
   update
 if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline-120:
   h_s,c_s=slsqp_full(best_h,best_n,min(time.time()+80,deadline-80), alpha=50000,maxiter=300)
   update
 if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline-20:
   h_loc,c_loc=local_pair(best_h,best_n,deadline-5)
   update
 # maybe try alt n if time left
 if time.time() < deadline-150:
   alt_n = 200 if best_n>=300 else 400
   # interpolate best to alt
   xold=np.linspace(0,2,best_n); xnew=np.linspace(0,2,alt_n)
   h_alt=project_feasible(np.interp(xnew,xold,best_h),alt_n)
   # quick lowdim M=8 and full/lbfgs? maybe too late.
   t_alt = min(time.time()+120, deadline-50)
   a_alt,h_alt_base,c_alt_base=opt_pure_cos(alt_n, min(time.time()+20,t_alt))
   if c_alt_base < c_alt? choose min
   lowdim? if time>80: lowdim_refine(alt_n,h_alt,a_alt,min(time+60,...))
   full? if time>40: lbfgs_full
   update
 # final project
 best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
 best_c=c5_max(best_h,best_n)
 return best_h, float(best_c), best_n
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, minimize_scalar


def c5_vec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)


def c5_max(h, n):
    return float(np.max(c5_vec(h, n)))


def smooth_val_w(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    e = np.exp(sh - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    w = e / s
    val = m + (mx + np.log(s)) / alpha
    return float(val), w


def grad_smooth(c, h, n, alpha, w=None):
    if w is None:
        _, w = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n)
    off = n - 1
    for idx in range(2 * n - 1):
        wi = w[idx]
        if wi < 1e-16:
            continue
        p = idx - off
        q = abs(p)
        L = n - q
        if L <= 0:
            continue
        wm = wi * dx
        if p >= 0:
            g[q:q + L] += wm * (1.0 - h[:L])
            g[:L] += -wm * h[q:q + L]
        else:
            g[:L] += wm * (1.0 - h[q:q + L])
            g[q:q + L] += -wm * h[:L]
    return g


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(250):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * room / tot
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * h / tot
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def pure_cos_basis(n):
    return np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n)


def pure_obj(a, n, base):
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * a * base
    return c5_max(h, n)


def opt_pure_cos(n, deadline):
    base = pure_cos_basis(n)
    a = 0.60
    try:
        res = minimize_scalar(pure_obj, bounds=(0.0, 1.0), args=(n, base),
                              method='bounded', options={'xatol': 1e-7})
        if np.isfinite(res.x):
            a = float(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0))
    except Exception:
        pass
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * a * base
    c = c5_max(h, n)
    return a, h, c


def build_basis(n, M):
    B = np.zeros((n, M))
    ang = 2.0 * np.pi * (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
    col = 0
    m = 1
    while col < M and 2 * m < n:
        if col < M:
            B[:, col] = np.cos(m * ang)
            col += 1
        if col < M:
            B[:, col] = np.sin(m * ang)
            col += 1
        m += 1
    return B[:, :col]


def scale_lowdim(B, a):
    if B.size == 0:
        return a
    u = B @ a
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
    if m > 1.0:
        a = a * (0.999 / m)
    return a


def lowdim_h(B, a):
    u = B @ a
    return 0.5 + 0.5 * u, u


def lowdim_obj(x, B, n, alpha, q):
    u = B @ x
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * u
    c = c5_vec(h, n)
    val, _ = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    over = np.maximum(0.0, u - 1.0)
    under = np.maximum(0.0, -1.0 - u)
    val += 0.25 * q * (float(np.dot(over, over)) + float(np.dot(under, under)))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e9
    return float(val)


def lowdim_jac(x, B, n, alpha, q):
    u = B @ x
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * u
    c = c5_vec(h, n)
    _, w = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    gh = grad_smooth(c, h, n, alpha, w)
    over = np.maximum(0.0, u - 1.0)
    under = np.maximum(0.0, -1.0 - u)
    du = 0.5 * q * (over - under)
    return 0.5 * (B.T @ (gh + du))


def lowdim_refine(n, start_h, a1, deadline):
    start_h = project_feasible(start_h, n)
    best_h = start_h.copy()
    best_c = c5_max(start_h, n)

    Bfull = build_basis(n, 16)
    if Bfull.shape[1] <= 0:
        return best_h, best_c
    B = Bfull

    try:
        a_fit = np.linalg.lstsq(B, 2.0 * start_h - 1.0, rcond=None)[0]
        a_fit = np.clip(a_fit, -2.0, 2.0)
        a_fit = scale_lowdim(B, a_fit)
    except Exception:
        a_fit = np.zeros(B.shape[1])
        if B.shape[1] > 0:
            a_fit[0] = a1

    a_pure = np.zeros(B.shape[1])
    if B.shape[1] > 0:
        a_pure[0] = a1
    a_pure = scale_lowdim(B, a_pure)

    for a0 in (a_fit, a_pure):
        h, _ = lowdim_h(B, a0)
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = c5_max(h, n)
        if c < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()
            a_fit = a0.copy()

    a_cur = a_fit.copy()
    seq = []
    for m in (2, 4, 8, 12, 16):
        if m <= B.shape[1]:
            seq.append(m)
    if not seq:
        seq = [B.shape[1]]

    settings = ((1000.0, 50.0, 70), (5000.0, 200.0, 90), (20000.0, 800.0, 110))

    for M in seq:
        Bs = B[:, :M]
        if a_cur is None or len(a_cur) == 0:
            a_use = np.zeros(M)
            if M > 0:
                a_use[0] = a1
        else:
            a_use = np.zeros(M)
            a_use[:min(M, len(a_cur))] = a_cur[:min(M, len(a_cur))]
        a_use = scale_lowdim(Bs, a_use)
        bounds = [(-2.0, 2.0)] * M

        for alpha, q, nit in settings:
            if time.time() > deadline - 1:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(lowdim_obj, a_use, jac=lowdim_jac,
                               args=(Bs, n, alpha, q),
                               method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                               options={'maxiter': nit, 'ftol': 1e-12,
                                        'gtol': 1e-10, 'maxls': 20})
                a_use = res.x
            except Exception:
                pass
            a_use = scale_lowdim(Bs, a_use)
            h, _ = lowdim_h(Bs, a_use)
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = c5_max(h, n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
                a_cur = a_use.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def full_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = c5_vec(x, n)
    val, _ = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    s = x.sum() - n / 2.0
    val += mu * s * s
    return float(val)


def full_jac(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = c5_vec(x, n)
    _, w = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    g = grad_smooth(c, x, n, alpha, w)
    s = x.sum() - n / 2.0
    g += 2.0 * mu * s
    return g


def full_obj_eq(x, n, alpha):
    c = c5_vec(x, n)
    val, _ = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    return float(val)


def full_jac_eq(x, n, alpha):
    c = c5_vec(x, n)
    _, w = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
    return grad_smooth(c, x, n, alpha, w)


def eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - n / 2.0)


def eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n)


def lbfgs_full(h, n, deadline):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    settings = ((500.0, 2.0, 70), (2000.0, 0.2, 90),
                (8000.0, 0.02, 120), (30000.0, 0.002, 150))
    for alpha, mu, nit in settings:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(full_obj, best_h, jac=full_jac,
                           args=(n, alpha, mu), method='L-BFGS-B',
                           bounds=bounds,
                           options={'maxiter': nit, 'ftol': 1e-13,
                                    'gtol': 1e-11, 'maxls': 30})
            h2 = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            c2 = c5_max(h2, n)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c


def slsqp_full(h, n, deadline, alpha=30000, maxiter=250):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, n)
    if time.time() > deadline - 5:
        return best_h, best_c
    constraints = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': eq_fun, 'jac': eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(full_obj_eq, best_h, jac=full_jac_eq, args=(n, alpha),
                       method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-13})
        h2 = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        c2 = c5_max(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            return h2, c2
    except Exception:
        pass
    return best_h, best_c


def local_pair(h, n, deadline):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, n)
    alphas = (2000.0, 10000.0, 40000.0)
    deltas = (0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005)
    K = 8

    for alpha in alphas:
        while time.time() < deadline - 1:
            c = c5_vec(best_h, n)
            _, w = smooth_val_w(c, alpha)
            g = grad_smooth(c, best_h, n, alpha, w)
            if n <= K:
                idx = np.arange(n)
            else:
                idx = np.arange(n)
            low = np.argpartition(g, K)[:K]
            high = np.argpartition(-g, K)[:K]
            improved = False
            accepted = 0
            for i in low:
                if accepted >= 3:
                    break
                for j in high:
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    for d in deltas:
                        hi = best_h[i] + d
                        lo = best_h[j] - d
                        if hi <= 1.0 and lo >= 0.0:
                            oi = best_h[i]
                            oj = best_h[j]
                            best_h[i] = hi
                            best_h[j] = lo
                            cn = c5_max(best_h, n)
                            if cn < best_c - 1e-13:
                                best_c = cn
                                improved = True
                                accepted += 1
                                break
                            else:
                                best_h[i] = oi
                                best_h[j] = oj
                    if accepted >= 3:
                        break
            if not improved:
                break
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(20.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    np.random.seed(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')
    best_n = None

    h_init = None
    n_init = 300
    try:
        h_init = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_init = len(h_init)
    except Exception:
        h_init = None

    if h_init is not None and len(h_init) > 0:
        if 60 <= n_init <= 450:
            n_use = n_init
            best_h = project_feasible(h_init, n_use)
        else:
            n_use = 300
            xold = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_init)
            xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_use)
            best_h = project_feasible(np.interp(xnew, xold, h_init), n_use)
    else:
        n_use = 300

    if best_h is not None:
        best_n = n_use
        best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n)

    a1 = 0.60
    if time.time() < deadline - 350:
        tp = min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 300)
        a1, hp, cp = opt_pure_cos(n_use, tp)
        if best_h is None:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = hp, n_use, cp
        elif cp < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = hp, n_use, cp

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 300:
        tl = min(time.time() + 260.0, deadline - 280)
        hl, cl = lowdim_refine(best_n, best_h, a1, tl)
        if cl < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h, best_c = hl, cl

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 260:
        alt_n = 200 if best_n >= 320 else 400
        if alt_n == best_n:
            alt_n = 300
        if alt_n != best_n:
            xold = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)
            xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, alt_n)
            ha = project_feasible(np.interp(xnew, xold, best_h), alt_n)
            ca = c5_max(ha, alt_n)
            ta = min(time.time() + 30.0, deadline - 220)
            a_alt, hpa, cpa = opt_pure_cos(alt_n, ta)
            if cpa < ca - 1e-14:
                ha, ca = hpa, cpa
            if time.time() < deadline - 140:
                tl2 = min(time.time() + 170.0, deadline - 130)
                hl2, cl2 = lowdim_refine(alt_n, ha, a_alt, tl2)
                if cl2 < ca - 1e-14:
                    ha, ca = hl2, cl2
            if ca < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = ha, alt_n, ca

    if best_h is not None:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n)

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 220:
        tf = deadline - 230
        hf, cf = lbfgs_full(best_h, best_n, tf)
        if cf < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h, best_c = hf, cf

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 100:
        ts = min(time.time() + 90.0, deadline - 70)
        hs, cs = slsqp_full(best_h, best_n, ts, alpha=30000, maxiter=220)
        if cs < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 15:
        tl = deadline - 4.0
        hc, cc = local_pair(best_h, best_n, tl)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_h, best_c = hc, cc

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 300
        best_h = np.full(best_n, 0.5)
        best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_max(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
